Creators follow the brand's brief to make short-form videos and post them to your own social accounts. Brands pay for the views your content generates. The more your content performs, the more you earn.\"},{\"id\":\"How much can I earn?\",\"question\":\"How much can I earn?\",\"answer\":\"Each campaign has a pay rate listed per thousand views (CPM). Once your post is approved and meets all requirements, you'll start earning at that rate until the campaign budget runs out. For example, if your post gains 100,000 views for a campaign with a $1 CPM, you earn $100.\"},{\"id\":\"How do I start?\",\"question\":\"How do I start?\",\"answer\":\"Sign up for free, connect your social accounts, and browse open campaigns. Pick a campaign, follow the brief, post your clip, and submit the link through Vyro.\"},{\"id\":\"How do I submit a clip?\",\"question\":\"How do I submit a clip?\",\"answer\":\"Once you've posted a clip to social media, head back to Vyro and find the campaign you're submitting to. Paste the link to your social media post and that's it! You'll get an email when your content has been reviewed and approved. You'll have to link your social account to Vyro before you submit.\"},{\"id\":\"When does my post start earning?\",\"question\":\"When does my post start earning?\",\"answer\":\"Views start counting as soon as you submit your post on Vyro - even before it's reviewed. Once your post is approved and reaches the minimum view threshold, you'll start earning. Your view count and pending earnings refresh hourly, and you earn at the campaign's CPM rate for every eligible view while the budget is still available. Earning stops when the campaign's budget runs out.\"},{\"id\":\"How do payouts work?\",\"question\":\"How do payouts work?\",\"answer\":\"While a campaign is active, your earnings are listed as 'pending'. Once the campaign ends, we run a final view verification check and release the funds to your Vyro wallet's available balance, which is ready to cash out. You can withdraw once you've earned a minimum of $10, once per week. Withdrawals are processed via Stripe or PayPal depending on your location.\"},{\"id\":\"Why was my post rejected?\",\"question\":\"Why was my post rejected?\",\"answer\":\"Posts are reviewed against the rules listed in the campaign brief and platform standards. The most common reasons are not following the campaign brief, missing the required ad disclosure, or low quality and spammy content. If your post is flagged for fraudulent or botted views, your account may be permanently banned from Vyro. If you choose to create your own, please keep it focused on the film. Captions should focus on the film\u0026#39;s suspense, emotional storytelling, breathtaking visuals, and the performances of its cast. \u003cu\u003eAvoid captions\u003c/u\u003e that introduce unrelated topics, references to cast members’ previous roles, controversial discussion, references to the cast\u0026#39;s personal lives, \u0026quot;thirst\u0026quot; or appearance-focused commentary about the cast, or negative sentiment.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u0026quot;This movie looks incredible.\u0026quot;\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u0026quot;Ridley Scott doesn\u0026#39;t miss.\u0026quot;\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u0026quot;Jacob Elordi was made for this role.\u0026quot;\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u0026quot;The visuals in this movie are unreal.\u0026quot;\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u0026quot;This is definitely going on my watchlist.\u0026quot;\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u0026quot;Can\u0026#39;t wait to see this in theaters.\u0026quot;\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eText instructions\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eMake sure your posts include the following text in the caption (or on screen):\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eThe movie title: “\u003cstrong\u003eThe Dog Stars”\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eRelease date: “\u003cstrong\u003eIn theaters August 28th”\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRules:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eThis campaign has strict content guidelines. Please follow all of the rules listed.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e1) Content Rules:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eClips must come exclusively from the provided content folders.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eNo unrelated footage or outside visuals.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eEdits should not misrepresent the original scene.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDo not use unauthorized Disney IP or characters from other Disney franchises.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDo not introduce new characters or visual elements outside of the film.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDo not reference or incorporate any third-party intellectual property, including characters, franchises, or brands from other games, movies, TV shows, or companies.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003ePrioritize suspense, emotion, scale, and cinematic visuals.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDo \u003cstrong\u003enot\u003c/strong\u003e reference or speculate about the personal lives or relationships of any cast members.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDo \u003cstrong\u003enot\u003c/strong\u003e create misleading edits or out-of-context clips that negatively portray the film or its characters.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eKeep messaging focused on the film itself and encourage audiences to experience The Dog Stars in theaters.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eAny unaffiliated watermarks and logos displayed on screen will be rejected.\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e2) Audio Rules:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eYou may use the native audio included in the provided content, provided that the music remains unaltered from the source clips (e.g. no remixes, remasters, arrangements, pitch/speed adjustments, display of lyrics, or mixes with other songs or sound effects).\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDo not let music bleed from one clip to another. Each clip’s audio must remain contained within the clip it originated from.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDo not use any unlicensed or non-commercial audio, sound effects, or music from other creators or platforms.\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e3) Editing Guidelines:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eLight cuts, pacing adjustments, and subtle zooms are allowed\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e4) Caption Requirements:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003e“The Dog Stars, in theaters August 28th”\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e5) Do not use unrelated or generic hashtags\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e6) Any variation from these rules may result in rejection\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003e\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ch3\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReminders\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eYour post’s likes and comments must be turned on. However, when posting on Instagram Reels, TikTok, or YouTube Shorts, do not permit audio sharing and do not turn on duetting, stitching, or remixing.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDo not make negative content or content that misrepresents Disney, 20th Century Studios, The Dog Stars, or it’s content.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDo not use any logos, hashtags, or content that is not affiliated with this campaign.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eClips running as paid ads will be rejected (e.g., boosting your post as a promoted ad).\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eWe review all posts for inauthentic engagement. Accounts flagged for artificial views or activity may be removed from the campaign.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eDue to a high volume of submissions, review times may take several days.
